<script type="text/javascript">
    function checkLoginState() {
        FB.login(function(response){
            receiveFBData(response.authResponse.accessToken);
        }, {
            scope:'public_profile,email,user_friends'
        });
    }

    function receiveFBData(token) {
        FB.api('/me', {fields: 'first_name,last_name,email,gender'},function(response) {
            var data = "firstName="+response.first_name+"&lastName="+response.last_name+"&id="+response.id+
                    "&gender="+response.gender+"&email="+response.email+"&token="+token;

            var goToValue = $("input[name='goTo']").attr('value');

            if (goToValue!=undefined){
                data+="&goTo="+encodeURIComponent(goToValue);
            }

            var inviteCodeValue = $("input[name='inviteCode']").attr('value');

            if (inviteCodeValue!=undefined){
                data+="&inviteCode="+encodeURIComponent(inviteCodeValue);
            }

            $.post("/account/facebook",
                data, function(r){
                    eval(r);
                    window.location.href = data.redirect;
                }
            ).fail(function() {
                window.location.href = "/es/login";
            });
        });
    }
</script>

            // Load the SDK asynchronously
            (function(d, s, id) {
                var js, fjs = d.getElementsByTagName(s)[0];
                if (d.getElementById(id)) return;
                js = d.createElement(s); js.id = id;
                js.src = "//connect.facebook.net/es_ES/sdk.js";
                fjs.parentNode.insertBefore(js, fjs);
            }(document, 'script', 'facebook-jssdk'));
        </script>
<!-- End Facebook SDK -->
<script type="text/javascript">
    function checkLoginState() {
        FB.login(function(response){
            receiveFBData(response.authResponse.accessToken);
        }, {
            scope:'public_profile,email,user_friends'
        });
    }

    function receiveFBData(token) {
        FB.api('/me', {fields: 'first_name,last_name,email,gender'},function(response) {
            var data = "firstName="+response.first_name+"&lastName="+response.last_name+"&id="+response.id+
                    "&gender="+response.gender+"&email="+response.email+"&token="+token;

            var goToValue = $("input[name='goTo']").attr('value');

            if (goToValue!=undefined){
                data+="&goTo="+encodeURIComponent(goToValue);
            }

            var inviteCodeValue = $("input[name='inviteCode']").attr('value');

            if (inviteCodeValue!=undefined){
                data+="&inviteCode="+encodeURIComponent(inviteCodeValue);
            }

            $.post("/account/facebook",
                data, function(r){
                    eval(r);
                    window.location.href = data.redirect;
                }
            ).fail(function() {
                window.location.href = "/es/login";
            });
        });
    }
</script>
